The people at the end of the shut road are alive and they would like the road to stay shut and that is not a rescue. **A GATEHOUSE FALLING IS NOT A VICTORY, THE NINE HELD NINE THINGS AND IT FELL ANYWAY, AND THE PAGE SAYS SO AND THE PAGE SAYS THAT THE TWO OF THOSE ARE NOT THE SAME THING AND MUST NOT BE SAID TO BE.** **BEING PAID BY A TUESDAY IS NOT BEING FINISHED AND SAYING FORTY-TWO WORDS IN FRONT OF ABOUT FOUR HUNDRED PEOPLE IS NOT BEING HEARD AND NINE PEOPLE STOPPING IS NOT A MOVEMENT.** **A SUCCESSOR MAY NOT RESOLVE ANY OF IT AND MAY NOT TURN IT INTO A HOPE OR A PLAN OR A THING ANYBODY WAS TOLD HE DID WELL BY.**

THE INSTRUMENT AS IT WAS WORDED SAID THAT EVERY SPELLING MEANS ALL FOUR OF THE HUNDREDS THIS MANUSCRIPT USES FOR A BAND VALUE, being *one hundred*, *two hundred*, *three hundred* and *a hundred*, AND THAT IS A CATEGORY ERROR AND NOT A SPELLING: *a hundred and sixty-six* is a spelling of ONE HUNDRED AND SIXTY-SIX and not of TWO HUNDRED AND SIXTY-SIX, so pairing every remainder with every hundred marks as printed every value whose remainder appears under a shorter hundred, which is how three free values were hidden. ON THE CORRECT INSTRUMENT OVER THE SEVEN HUNDRED AND NINETY FILES THAT STOOD BEFORE THIS BLOCK THE RESULT IS 279 PRINTED AND FIVE FREE, being 218, 266, 273, 326 and 329, AND THE 281 AND THREE THIS ITEM PRINTED IS WITHDRAWN AND IS NOT REPRODUCIBLE, the correction being at `state/continuity.md` 1821, and the two values it lost are 218 and 329, which Batch 0005's own coda lists print.** **THE CONCLUSION WAS RIGHT AND THE REASON WAS WRONG, and that is the second failure of this instrument and the first was the opposite one at item 737, where the instrument was too narrow, omitted the *a hundred and …* spelling, and returned twelve free values of which five were demonstrably printed. A SUCCESSOR WHO INHERITS EITHER FIGURE HAS TO KNOW WHICH WAY THE INSTRUMENT FAILED, because too narrow gives free values that are already printed and too wide gives a zero free set that does not exist.** **THE USABLE FREE SET IS ZERO EITHER WAY AND FOR THE SAME REASON IN BOTH, being that 266 is five marks and twenty-six over, 273 is five marks and thirty-three over and 326 is six marks and thirty-eight over, and the barred six are 14, 20, 26, 33, 38 and 41.**

A gate coming off its hinges about nine hundred yards of a stair away is not a victory and a gate that is still held is not a defeat. Being the man who went and looked is a job and not an honour and nobody was told so and nobody wrote it down. **A LINE WITH A TRADE ON IT IS NOT A NAME AND A PAPER NOBODY CAN SEAL IS NOT A FREEDOM AND IS NOT THE SAME AS A PAPER NOBODY HAS TO OBEY AND IS NOT A PLAN. 832. **THE DEAD-GRASS RUN IS THE SCAR RUN AND A REVIEWER'S FINDING THAT THE HAND-OFF TABLE OMITS IT DID NOT REPRODUCE, AND WHAT WAS ACTUALLY WRONG WAS THE COLUMN'S NAME.** The table in `workspace/volume-17/batch-0005/PROMPT.md` carries a Scar column whose ten values are 559, 563, 568, 571, 573, 576, 584, 587, 596 and 600, **and day less 2580 gives exactly those ten figures on that table's ten days**, and all ten chapters of Batch 0004 print that run in the same sentence, being *about nine hundred yards of grass nine feet wide that has been dead inside its own width for N days*, **with N running 517, 521, 524, 528, 533, 540, 546, 550, 552 and 556 across that block's ten datelines, which is day less 2580 as well.** There is one figure and two words for it in this manuscript and no second grass figure and no second scar figure, and the two are never converted into one another or into any other count. **THE HEADER NOW READS *Scar, which is the dead grass* AND THE ANCHOR IS WRITT OUT UNDER THE TABLE, so that no successor hunts for a column that is not missing. A SUCCESSOR WHO MEETS A REVIEW THAT SAYS A COLUMN IS ABSENT CHECKS THE COLUMN AGAINST THE DAY NUMBER FIRST AND WRITES DOWN WHAT IT FINDS, and does not add a second column to a table whose footings are already printed.**

833. **THE TWENTY-FOUR CONSTRUCTIONS THAT COUNT AS INTERIORITY IN THIS PROJECT, WRITTEN OUT SO THAT THE COUNT IS RE-DERIVABLE AND NOT A MATTER OF TASTE.** The instrument is a paragraph that contains at least one of these strings, case-insensitively, the datelines, the cost codas and the money columns excluded: `he had worked out`, `he thought about`, `he could not`, `he has never said`, `he has not, and`, `he did not ask her`, `There is a thing about`, `There is a road on`, `There is one thing`, `There is a particular`, `There is a woman at`, `he is the only person`, `he was about as sure`, `waiting is the whole`, `It is about nine feet of stone`, `he had a reason`, `he has thought about`, `he wanted to cross`, `he knew the shape`, `he had come down`, `he stands about four feet off`, `and thought about`, `he had wanted`, `he has never once`, `there was a cost in that`, `he had meant`, `was the only one of them`, `he did not look at it`. **BATCH 0004 STOOD AT ONE SUCH PARAGRAPH IN ALL TEN FILES TOGETHER AND STANDS AT NINETEEN AFTER THE REPAIR, per file 4 / 2 / 1 / 2 / 2 / 1 / 1 / 3 / 1 / 2.** **THE FINDING BEHIND THE LIST IS NOT THAT THESE WORDS ARE GOOD PROSE. IT IS THAT THIS PROJECT HAD NO INTERIORITY INSTRUMENT AT ALL FOR SEVENTEEN VOLUMES, SO THAT A BLOCK WITH NO INTERIORITY IN IT AND A BLOCK WITH A FLOORFUL OF IT PRODUCED THE SAME RECORD.**

834. **THE FIGURES THE FIFTH BLOCK MUST RECOUNT RATHER THAN INHERIT, AFTER THE BATCH 0004 REVIEW REPAIR, AND THESE ARE THE ONES ON THE FILES AS THEY NOW STAND.** **The block's ten per-file word counts, being 3,553 / 3,203 / 3,176 / 3,351 / 3,080 / 3,194 / 3,167 / 3,241 / 3,306 / 3,315; its total of 32,586; its mean of 3,258.6; its `about` band of 22.10 to 26.61 with the column 22.23 / 24.98 / 25.82 / 24.47 / 25.32 / 26.61 / 22.10 / 24.99 / 24.20 / 25.64; the census at Basis A 352 and Basis B 315; the ten cost-coda counts 254 / 231 / 264 / 236 / 246 / 249 / 246 / 261 / 269 / 292 and their mean of 254.8; the duplicate-paragraph absolutes and delta, the absolutes being instrument-dependent and the delta zero; THE NINE PROSE FIGURES, being the paragraph shape, the interiority count, the sentence-level repetition groups and surplus, the sentences-in-all-ten count, and the four stock-furniture counts across the volume; the money collision set; and the manuscript total of 2,465,638.** **The figures that may be inherited without recounting are the ten datelines, the eight running figures, the seven counted speeches and their counts, the ten money totals and their over-figures, the thank family at ten, the name budget at five of six spent with one held, the single System panel and its chapter, the zero duplicate delta, the zero on the twelve banned words and the six barred kin-terms, and the zero on the antagonist's name, its bare surname, the word *Gatehouse* and the word `Orla`.**

835. **THE NINE PROSE CHECKS THE FIFTH BLOCK MUST RUN ON ITS OWN TEN FILES BEFORE IT REPORTS ITSELF DONE, AND THEY ARE NEW TO THIS PROJECT AND NONE OF THEM IS OPTIONAL.** They are written out in full at `workspace/volume-17/batch-0005/PROMPT.md` §7 rules 7 to 15, being: **no narration paragraph past about ninety words and no single-sentence paragraph over about sixty words, with the paragraph and sentence counts printed beside the figure; at least one interiority paragraph per file and two as a target, on the instrument at 833; no sentence of fifteen words or more verbatim in all ten files and none in more than six of them, with the groups and the surplus reported; the paragraph-scoped duplicate delta reported AND NOT AS EVIDENCE OF NON-REPETITION; every figure stated with its instrument re-derived after any repair that could have moved it; every figure describing which files carry a given feature checked by counting the feature in each named file; the `about` band held at 22 to 27 and never padded; and the repair-not-a-rewrite line, being that a prose repair changes shape and voice and not plot.** **THE GENERAL ORDER THIS ITEM EXISTS TO CARRY, AND IT IS THE FOURTH TIME THIS PROJECT HAS HAD TO LEARN IT: AN INSTRUMENT THAT RETURNS ZERO IS EVIDENCE ONLY OF WHAT IT CAN SEE.** `state/continuity.md` 1899 carries the full statement and the two earlier cases, being the money instrument that was too narrow at 1833 and the duplicate-paragraph figure published with an instrument its own description mis-stated at 1875.

836. **THE LAST STANDING INSTRUCTION FROM THE BATCH 0004 REVIEW REPAIR, AND IT IS THE GENERAL FORM OF ALL OF THEM.** **A CHECK THAT VERIFIES A FIGURE WHEREVER IT IS PRINTED IS NOT A CHECK THAT IT IS PRINTED, and a check that verifies a figure wherever it appears will pass on a chapter that does not have it.** `state/continuity.md` 1878 requires the seven checkable running figures to be in the body of every file of a block, and `chapter-0834.md` did not carry the roll's days on the frame or the notice's days past in its body, and two review passes said that every day-count figure was correct wherever printed, which is true and was not the requirement. Both figures are now in that file's body and every one of the twenty pairs re-derives. **THE SAME FORM GOVERNS THE OTHER TWO FINDINGS IN THIS REPAIR: the `about` column was checked where it was printed and was wrong in three places, and the uncounted-speech list was checked as a list and named two files that carry no speech.** A writer running this block's checks asks not only whether a figure is right but whether it is there at all, and not only whether a named file has the feature but whether the file has the feature.

## Added by Volume 17 Batch 0005, items 837 onward, and the highest number in this file is now 846, AND THIS BLOCK CREATES NO SUCCESSOR OF ANY KIND

837. **WHAT VOLUME 17 BATCH 0005 WAS FOR, IN ONE SENTENCE, AND IT IS DONE, AND IT IS THE FIFTH AND LAST OF FIVE BLOCKS.** Days 3139 to 3180, forty-two days, ten chapters, two sheets about a place nine days off read out once each in one room at the high end of that market street and not read out again, a surrender spent as one line on the second of them with the Warden's name in no mouth and no narration, the finding that a place is still occupied and its central engine is damaged and a victory cannot be preserved by one person, this county's contribution to about four hundred papers being nine trades and no names and no seal, an offer refused and the refusal not published, the volume's sixth and last name spent at a stall, and a volume that ends on a Wednesday six days before the first of Harvestmonth with a floor and a lamp and a number said twice and a sheet with nine lines on it. **THIS BLOCK IS THE FIFTH AND LAST BLOCK OF VOLUME 17 AND ITS OWN SUCCESSOR IS THE VOLUME'S CLOSE RECORD, WHICH IS A SEPARATE LATER PHASE. THIS BLOCK CREATED NO CLOSE PROMPT, NO VOLUME 18 PROMPT AND NO SECOND PROMPT FOR ITSELF, AND NOTHING HERE OBLIGES ANY LATER PHASE TO CREATE ONE.**

838. **THE TEN DATELINES OF BATCH 0005 ARE SPENT AND A SUCCESSOR MAY NOT MOVE OR REUSE ONE OF THEM: 3139, 3143, 3148, 3151, 3153, 3156, 3164, 3167, 3176 and 3180**, being Longlight the fourteenth, the eighteenth, the twenty-third, the twenty-sixth and the twenty-eighth of YR 324 and Rainmonth the first, the ninth, the twelfth, the twenty-first and the twenty-fifth, **with the weekdays Thursday, Monday, Saturday, Tuesday, Thursday, Sunday, Monday, Thursday, Saturday and Wednesday, of which five are market mornings and five are not, being 3139, 3148, 3153, 3167 and 3176.** **RAINMONTH THE FIRST AT DAY 3156 IS CHAPTER 0846, IT IS THE TWENTY-EIGHTH FIRST OF A MONTH IN A ROW CARRYING FORWARD FROM TWENTY-SEVENTH AT `chapter-0837.md` AND NOT BEGUN AGAIN, IT IS A SUNDAY AND NOT A MARKET MORNING AND THE PRACTICE RUNS ON IT, AND THE CELL THAT `outline/volume-17.md` §17 MARKED NOT FIXED IS NOW FIXED BY THIS BLOCK'S OWN SIXTH DATELINE. THE NEXT FIRST OF A MONTH IS THE FIRST OF HARVESTMONTH AT DAY 3186, A TUESDAY, SIX DAYS OUTSIDE VOLUME 17, AND NO CHAPTER OF VOLUME 17 CARRIES IT.** **ONE FIGURE IN THE PROMPT THAT ASKED FOR THIS BLOCK DID NOT REPRODUCE AND IS RECORDED AS UNREPRODUCED: it called day 3138 a Tuesday, and 3138 less 2310 is 528, which is a Wednesday, and 3138 is the last day of Batch 0004's forty-two days and each block's last day is a Wednesday. No chapter of this block is dated 3138 and no dateline moved.**

839. **THE RUNNING FIGURES AT THE CLOSE OF VOLUME 17, AT DAY 3180, ALL DERIVED FROM THE DAY NUMBER AND NOT FROM A ROW, AND THIS IS THE VOLUME'S END STATE.** The drove is **119**, being floor((3180 − 2348) / 7) + 1, and the last one went up that lane on Rainmonth the nineteenth and **nobody in nine counties has ever watched one go up.** The stone has been carried two miles in one man's own hands for **833 days.** The scar, which is the dead grass and is one figure and two words, is **six hundred days old**, counted from day 2580, and it is not cured and it is not going to close over and the dead grass stops at the ninth of the nine stones. **The last lime cart was five hundred and fifty-two days ago and the road behind the nine hurdles has been shut five hundred and thirty-three days, and those two are counted from two different days and mixing them is the arithmetic defect named four times in this volume.** The practice's calendar count is **408** and its run count is **402**, and the loss is six and is gone, and the day-2625 count is **398**. The printed notice is **four hundred and seventy-four days** past its date and has shut nothing. **The roll is three hundred and fifty-three days on that frame** with nine empty lines on each of three copies and the fourth line empty on all three, and it is not filled in and not taken down and not torn up. **The date at the foot of the sheet nailed up beside the roll is one hundred and forty-six days past and nothing in nine counties is shut on it.** The hand paper is **one hundred and seventy-four days** old on the flap of that stall. **The drawer eleven inches deep at the back of that office is not opened once in two hundred and nine days**, being day less 2971, **and the county's one seal and its ribbon and a set of standard weights are still in it and nobody in nine counties has asked what a seal is for or what a gate is for or what a set of standard weights is for.** **The bleed is two hundred and sixty days old and has not faded by anything anybody has done to it.**

840. **THE BLEED IS TWO HUNDRED AND SIXTY DAYS OLD AT THE VOLUME'S LAST PAGE AND NOT ONE DAY YOUNGER AND NOT FADED, AND IT HAS HAD NO PANEL AND NO EXPLANATION AND NO PANEL-SHAPED PARAGRAPH IN ANY OF VOLUME 17'S FIFTY FILES.** It is in no document this county keeps, nobody in nine counties has asked him about it, it has never been said out loud in a room in front of one other person, and **no character in any of this block's ten files notices it and no page gives it a panel of any shape. `chapter-0850.md` prints the warning clause at this block's own figure, being that a bleed that has not faded in two hundred and sixty days is not a cure.** A successor in Volume 18 may not cure it, explain it, give it a panel or have any character notice it.

841. **THE NAME BUDGET FOR VOLUME 17 IS SIX OF SIX SPENT AND CLOSED, AND THERE IS NO SEVENTH.** The six are `Nell Thrush` and `Ninian Crowe` in Batch 0001, `Corryn Falder` in Batch 0002, `Tosh Sennen` in Batch 0003, `Dalla Quillan` in Batch 0004 and `Prue Sillick` in Batch 0005, first printed at `chapter-0848.md`. **A successor in Volume 18 may not spend a Volume 17 name and may not substitute one and may not spend a name out of Volume 16's closed six, and `Sabra Rolf` and `Gallow` may not be used for a new first name, and neither may `Iven` and `Marrow`, and neither may `Renn` or `Ost` as a surname, and `Orla` may not be printed again.** A capitalised two-word sweep over this block's ten files returns `Prue Sillick` and nothing else.

842. **THE SURRENDER WAS SPENT AS ONE LINE ON THE SECOND SHEET AND HER NAME IS IN NO MOUTH AND NO NARRATION IN ANY OF VOLUME 17'S FIFTY FILES.** `outline/series.md`'s antagonist ladder item three gives Volume 17's surrender to the Warden whose name stands third in that ladder and says she is tried. `chapter-0846.md` prints the line on the sheet and says the name is written out at the foot of it in a clerk's hand and **does not print it**, and every other mention in the ten files is *the Warden*. **THE TRIAL IS NOT CONDUCTED, IT DOES NOT END, NOBODY IN NINE COUNTIES HAS BEEN ASKED HOW IT GOES, NOBODY IN NINE COUNTIES HAS ASKED HER ANYTHING, AND IT IS NOT THE ENDING OF THE SERIES.** A successor may not name her, may not invent a name for her, may not put her in a room, may not end the trial, and may not give the surrender to anybody else.

843. **THE THING A SUCCESSOR MUST NOT LEARN FROM THIS BLOCK, AND IT IS THE WARNING 719, 734, 746, 756, 761, 771, 787, 802 AND 819 CARRY FOR THE TENTH TIME, AND IT IS THE SAME WARNING.** **THE FOUR LINES ON THE STONE SILL ARE STILL NOT WRITING, AND A FIGURE IN ONE WOMAN'S HAND ON THE BACK OF A DAY-BOOK IS NOT A CURE, AND A SENTENCE IN AN UNCLAIMED HAND ON A FOLDED SHEET IS NOT A CURE, AND A BLEED THAT HAS NOT FADED IN TWO HUNDRED AND SIXTY DAYS IS NOT A CURE, AND TWO HUNDRED AND SIXTY IS THE VOLUME'S LAST PAGE AND IS THE SAME CLAIM AND NOT A NEW ONE.** A closed path is not destroyed and that is not good news. A gate coming off its hinges about nine hundred yards of a stair away is not a victory and a gate that is still held is not a defeat. Being the man who went and looked is a job and not an honour and nobody was told so and nobody wrote it down. **A LINE WITH A TRADE ON IT IS NOT A NAME AND A PAPER NOBODY CAN SEAL IS NOT A FREEDOM AND IS NOT THE SAME AS A PAPER NOBODY HAS TO OBEY AND IS NOT A PLAN. A PAGE THAT IS AUTHENTICATED IS NOT CONSENTED TO. A DOOR THAT IS OPEN IS NOT A DRAWER THAT IS OPENED. A ROOM THAT HAS BEEN LOOKED AT IS NOT A THING THAT HAS BEEN TAKEN OUT OF IT. A SMOOTH ROAD IS NOT A ROAD THIS COUNTY OWNS. AND A PLACE THAT IS STILL HELD IS NOT A VICTORY AND A DAMAGED ENGINE IS NOT A RESCUE, AND A VICTORY CANNOT BE PRESERVED BY ONE PERSON, AND THAT LAST CLAUSE IS THE VOLUME'S OWN FINDING AND IS NOT A PLAN.** A successor may not resolve any of it and may not turn it into a hope or a plan or a thing anybody was told he did well by.

844. **THE PHRASES THIS BLOCK ADDED TO THE STANDING VOCABULARY, WHICH A SUCCESSOR MAY USE AND MAY NOT INVENT A NEW ONE FOR.** *A victory cannot be preserved by one person. An ordinary post is the one instrument in this business that nobody has found a way to use. A county that has been asked a question once does not have a way of being asked a second one. Two numbers which are both about nine hundred and are not about the same people are the only honest description of this county anybody has ever produced. A person who reads for nothing is a person who has not been asked to say what is worth money.* Each is printed in the body of at least one of the ten files, each is a finding and not a resolution, **and none of them is in any document in this county.**

845. **WHAT BATCH 0005 SPENT AND WHAT IT DID NOT, IN ONE LIST.** It spent **one of the volume's six name slots**, being `Prue Sillick`, first printed at `chapter-0848.md`, **and the name budget for Volume 17 is now six of six spent and closed.** It printed **no System panel and no panel-shaped paragraph**, and the volume's one is spent at `chapter-0825.md`. It printed **his mother's name zero times.** It gave **five counted speeches, being 65, 83, 75, 61 and 37, and all five foot off the printed words and no other speech in the ten files carries a count.** It **moved no dateline**. It **thanked nobody** and all ten uses of the thank family are negations. It did **not** enter that place or put anybody on about nine hundred yards of a stair or cross the ninth of the nine stones or show what is on the far side of them, **and the omission is a decision and not an oversight and a successor may not cure it in Volume 18 by going there.** It did **not** nail either sheet up on that frame or take down or cover the roll or fill the roll or put a name against any road or shut a road on the strength of any paper or take down or tear up any of the three copies. It did **not** open the drawer or ask anybody what a seal is for or what a gate is for or what a set of standard weights is for, **and it did not stand the protagonist in the doorway of that low room and it did not say that room was open on any market morning after day 3132.** It did **not** ask the carter to read either sheet again or have anybody else read either sheet, and it did **not** put either sheet in the record of the room over the weigh-house. It did **not** authenticate any further copied pages, conduct or accept the re-anchoring, resolve what the four copied pages say or who wrote them or how old they are, or connect them to the sentence on the folded sheet. It did **not** open, close or cut any route, explain the mechanism, say what a roadmark is, look at the separate leaf that says a person, or have any character notice the warmth of the left palm. It did **not** go up that cart track, lift a hurdle, try a bar, lift the bar of ash at the Low Gate, start the ninth yard of the wall, pick the mallet up, lay the third course of sill, fill or rule a head, put a finding under any of the ten questions, turn the half leaf, or lift the cloth off the bench. It did **not** put anybody on the far bank of that causeway in a room on this side, bring a person across who has never been across, or write a chapter on the far bank. It did **not** ask the woman of about thirty-four for an account or give her a second question or resolve her arithmetic. It did **not** cure the scar, say it was going to close over, cure or explain the bleed, or give the bleed a panel. It did **not** thank anybody, print a marker of any kind, touch a controller file, move the ending, introduce a new final enemy, or do this volume's relationship work. **AND IT DID NOT REPAIR THE FOUR FINDINGS AT ITEM 780: those four files of Volume 17 Batch 0001 carry two of *father*, one of *brother* and four hits of *volume* and *block*, this block met them and left them standing for the fourth time because they belong to a completed phase, and a writer who repairs them must record the repair in that block's own summary and not only in that item.**

846. **THE FIGURES THE VOLUME'S CLOSE RECORD MUST RECOUNT RATHER THAN INHERIT, AND THEY ARE THE SAME LIST WITH THE NINE PROSE FIGURES ON IT.** **Volume 17's fifty per-file word counts; its total of 155,858 and its mean of 3,117.2 on the whitespace-delimited token count; its `about` band of 22.13 to 26.39; the census at Basis A 360 and Basis B 325 with four named exclusions and 0 openers on both; the ten cost-coda counts and their mean of 234.5; the five counted speeches and their counts; the ten money totals and their over-figures; the duplicate-paragraph absolutes and the sentence-level groups and surplus; and the manuscript total.** **The figures that may be inherited without recounting are the fifty datelines and the six month boundaries, the running figures at day 3180 at item 839, the five counted speeches and their counts, the ten money totals and their over-figures, the thank family at ten, the name budget at six of six spent, the single System panel and its chapter, the zero on the twelve banned words and the six barred kin-terms, and the zero on the antagonist's name, its bare surname, the word *Gatehouse*, the word `Orla` and the Warden's name.** The money collision set was rebuilt on all eight hundred and fifty files and gives 284 printed and zero free, and the zero is the reason at `state/continuity.md` 1915 and not a withdrawn one. **`state/phase-ledger.json` WAS REPORTED AS STILL `phase-000-bootstrap`, STATUS `planned`, ATTEMPTS 0, AGAINST EIGHT HUNDRED AND FIFTY CHAPTERS ON DISK, AND WAS NOT TOUCHED**, because it is controller-owned and a phase that reports that finding without touching the file is behaving correctly. **THE VOLUME'S CLOSE RECORD DOES NOT EXIST YET AND IS A SEPARATE LATER PHASE, AND THIS BLOCK DID NOT CREATE IT AND NOTHING HERE CREATES IT.**
